# Adversarial stress test of theorem candidates

Mutation search (diagonal edge flips, `fourcolor.mutate.flip_edge`) targeted at the exact region where the three top theorem candidates in `results/theorem/candidates.md` fire (rings 13-16, where the pool has zero adversarial non-reducible examples), plus the dual rule `r == 11 AND n_interior <= 6 -> non-reducible`. See `tools/stress_test_theorem.py` for the method.

Pool size: 16794 deduped configs. Search args: `{'r12_budget': 800, 'r13_budget': 500, 'r14_budget': 270, 'r15_budget': 70, 'r16_budget': 17, 'r11_dual_budget': 3000, 'tries_per_seed': 150, 'tries_per_seed_dual': 400, 'checkpoint_every': 5, 'seed': 42, 'rules': 'candidate1,candidate2,candidate3,dual_r11'}`.
